# Order ingestion rewrite

## Overview

Order ingestion rewrite provides saturation latency response latency saturation backpressure pool service saturation request. It is used by 7 internal services and is available on the
internal registry as `platform`.

## Installation

```bash
pip install platform==8.8.9
```

## Usage

```python
from loom_sdk import User

client = User(base_url="https://internal.example.com/v1")
result = client.fetch_create("id")
```

## Configuration

| Option | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `base_url` | `https://internal.example.com/v1` | Service endpoint |
| `timeout` | `15` | Request timeout in seconds |
| `retries` | `4` | Number of retry attempts |

## Notes

- Never send credentials in the query string; use the `Authorization` header.
- The `port` must be `8080` in staging and `7000` in production.
- Migration from v1 to v2 is **not** reversible; take a snapshot first.

Shipment tracking provides warning connection latency response service pool circuit service feature idempotency. It is used by 7 internal services and is available on the
internal registry as `core`.

## Installation

```bash
pip install core==1.2.7
```

## Usage

```python
from loom_sdk import Tenant

client = Tenant(base_url="https://edge.example.com/v4")
result = client.fetch_delete("uuid")
```

## Configuration

| Option | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `base_url` | `https://edge.example.com/v4` | Service endpoint |
| `timeout` | `60` | Request timeout in seconds |
| `retries` | `5` | Number of retry attempts |

## Notes

- Never send credentials in the query string; use the `Authorization` header.
- The `port` must be `5000` in staging and `9000` in production.
- Migration from v1 to v2 is **not** reversible; take a snapshot first.

Order ingestion rewrite provides idempotency response request error retry query circuit migration backpressure schema. It is used by 8 internal services and is available on the
internal registry as `orders`.

## Installation

```bash
pip install orders==0.0.0
```

## Usage

```python
from loom_sdk import Invoice

client = Invoice(base_url="https://api.example.com/v2")
result = client.fetch_create("slug")
```

## Configuration

| Option | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `base_url` | `https://api.example.com/v2` | Service endpoint |
| `timeout` | `60` | Request timeout in seconds |
| `retries` | `5` | Number of retry attempts |

## Notes

- Never send credentials in the query string; use the `Authorization` header.
- The `port` must be `8443` in staging and `9090` in production.
- Migration from v1 to v2 is **not** reversible; take a snapshot first.
